Remaining math-copy flags

227 expressions; each appears once. Source-review candidates and conversion handling are distinguished below. Flags are not necessarily textbook errors.

70 reviewed nuclear-notation expressions need converter support, not correction of their empty-base scripts. Any other flags on these expressions remain listed. Textbook links open the published preview; expressions below use current local source.

Conversion handling: multiline layout (130) · Conversion handling: Unicode fallback approved (1) · Conversion handling: Greek symbol names and fallback (15) · Conversion handling: Unicode fallback approved (1) · ASCIIMath renderer compatibility (10) · Supported with package caveat (1) · Conversion handling: valid nuclear notation (69) ·

Conversion handling: multiline layout

LaTeX aligned/array can preserve the layout. ASCIIMath supports invisible-delimiter matrix layouts, but column alignment is less portable. Prefer whole-expression LaTeX plus an explicitly chosen ASCIIMath row layout or line-by-line copy.

Section 12.7 · eip-168 · m67807-math-0063

Supported with package caveat

ASCIIMath cancel(...) is supported. LaTeX \cancel{...} usually needs the cancel package; advertise that requirement instead of dropping cancellation marks.

equation 0.3.1 · m67032-math-0049

Supported with package caveat

Open textbook passage

80m×1km1000m=0.080 km.
Source MathML
<ns0:math xmlns:ns0="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><ns0:semantics><ns0:mrow><ns0:mrow><ns0:mrow><ns0:mn>80</ns0:mn> <ns0:menclose notation="updiagonalstrike"><ns0:mtext>m</ns0:mtext></ns0:menclose><ns0:mrow><ns0:mrow><ns0:mo stretchy="false">×</ns0:mo><ns0:mfrac><ns0:mrow><ns0:mn>1</ns0:mn><ns0:mspace width="0.167em" /><ns0:mtext>km</ns0:mtext></ns0:mrow><ns0:mrow><ns0:mn>1000</ns0:mn><ns0:menclose notation="updiagonalstrike"><ns0:mtext>m</ns0:mtext></ns0:menclose></ns0:mrow></ns0:mfrac></ns0:mrow><ns0:mo stretchy="false">=</ns0:mo><ns0:mn>0</ns0:mn></ns0:mrow><ns0:mtext>.080 km.</ns0:mtext></ns0:mrow></ns0:mrow><ns0:mrow /></ns0:mrow><ns0:annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"80"" m" times  {  {"1 km"}  over  {"1000 m"} } =0 "." "080"`"km"} {}</ns0:annotation></ns0:semantics></ns0:math>

Conversion handling: valid nuclear notation

No source correction is required for the empty-base nuclear scripts. Preserve the mass/atomic numbers as left scripts on the following element. LaTeX supports an empty base, for example {}^{258}\mathrm{Ha}; ASCIIMath can use an empty group for prescripts. Test the target renderer when implementing copying. Other independent flags, if any, remain applicable.
